([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/tabular-deep-embed-clustering-eval) · ★3)
- **tabular-deep-learning-inference**: Use when a query AnnData (.h5ad) dataset has a different number/order of genes than the reference model's training panel, and you need cell-type label predictions from a pretrained SIMS TabNet checkpoint without feature-mismatch errors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tabular-deep-learning-inference))
- **tabular-deep-learning-model-training**: Use when training or reproducing deep tabular models (scTab/TabNet) for cross-tissue cell type classification on the CELLxGENE census corpus, or benchmarking them against linear/XGBoost/MLP baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tabular-deep-learning-model-training))
- **tabular-document-review**: Extract structured data from multiple documents into comparison matrix with citations. Use for bulk document review. ([borghei/Claude-Skills](https://github.com/borghei/Claude-Skills/tree/HEAD/legal/tabular-document-review) · ★1.2k · 4 copies)
- **tabular-document-reviewer**: Runs a structured review over a set of documents of any type (notices, FIRs, judgments, invoices, correspondence, KYC and due-diligence files), with one row per document and one column per question, every cell citing the document page or paragraph or marked not found, and output as CSV or XLSX for spot-checking. Use when a lawyer says "put these in a spreadsheet", "extract the same fields from all these files" or "review this bundle as a table". Do not use for contract term abstraction (use contract-summariser) or a foreign firm's coding protocol (use lpo-document-review-coder). ([AdvRahulAR/dharmabot-legal-skills-v1](https://github.com/AdvRahulAR/dharmabot-legal-skills-v1/tree/HEAD/plugins/dharmabot-legal-skills/skills/tabular-document-reviewer) · ★1)
- **tabular-ds-protocol**: The non-negotiable protocol for a tabular ML study — deterministic split first, baseline before tuned models, document the target and metric, leakage review, compare to baseline, produce a model card. Load at the start of any Labmate study. ([amazingvince/Labmate](https://github.com/amazingvince/Labmate/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/tabular-ds-protocol))
- **tabular-eda**: Explore and explain local tabular CSV or Parquet datasets, including data quality, distributions, relationships, optional target behavior, time coverage, and group structure. Use when Codex is asked for exploratory data analysis, dataset profiling, data understanding, or an EDA report without model training. ([js1972/codex-ml-skill](https://github.com/js1972/codex-ml-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/tabular-eda) · ★1)
- **tabular-eda**: Profile a new tabular dataset before modeling. Find target leakage, missing data patterns, high-cardinality categoricals, near-constant features, redundant pairs, and non-linear relationships that Pearson correlation misses. Use whenever the user hands you a CSV or parquet and asks "what should I do with this?" Always run this skill before training any model on data you haven't seen before. ([brojonat/manager-packs](https://github.com/brojonat/manager-packs/tree/HEAD/bundles/tabular-eda))
- **tabular-eda-ann**: Performs comprehensive Exploratory Data Analysis (EDA) on tabular CSV datasets using pandas, seaborn, and matplotlib. ([notcis/PyTorch-DeepLearning](https://github.com/notcis/PyTorch-DeepLearning/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/tabular-eda-ann))
- **tabular-eda-review**: Inspect CSV datasets for schema, quality, modeling readiness, and feature analysis. ([PracticalSwan/agent-skills](https://github.com/PracticalSwan/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/tabular-eda-review) · 2 copies)
- **tabular-editor-bpa**: Create, validate, and audit Tabular Editor Best Practice Analyzer rules for Power BI semantic models. - **tabular-feature-intensity-export**: Use when a completed OpenMS feature-linking step (FeatureLinkerUnlabeledQT) has produced aligned feature maps across replicate LC-MS runs and the intensities must be exported to a tabular text file for statistical analysis. - **tabular-feature-selection-eval**: Evaluates feature selection methods by measuring downstream neural network performance on tabular datasets containing controlled extraneous features. It probes whether selected features improve or maintain predictive accuracy for classification and reduce error for regression tasks. Use when the user wants to benchmark on ALOI (AL), California Housing (CA), Covertype (CO), Eye Movements (EY), Gesture (GE), Helena (HE), Higgs 98k (HI), House 16K (HO), Jannis (JA), Otto Group Product Classification (OT), Year (YE), Microsoft (MI), or asks about evaluating this task. - **tabular-field-discovery**: Tabular field and schema discovery skill. Use when the user uploads or references structured tabular files (markdown tables, CSV, Excel exports, plain-text grids) and wants to identify, map, or align similar columns/fields across one or more files — even when column names differ slightly. Best for merge/join prep, schema comparison, and data extraction. For downstream analysis or visualization use the appropriate data or chart skill after field discovery is complete.
